HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001653/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_19042a27ac1f27306921","text":"2. **A promised dwelling reversed into compulsory dwelling.** Fixed `مُّؤْصَدَةٌ` activates `و ص د B002`, the forecourt or doorway joined to a dwelling. Within the focus window, `104:3` supplies wealth (`م و ل B001`) and clinging or settling (`خ ل د B002`), while `104:4` supplies involuntary casting. The packet gives a permanence-claim, displacement, and an attached doorway; I infer a reversal from property-backed residence to residence without freedom of threshold. The closure reads as a grim house-relation: the person who expected wealth to stabilize a dwelling is made to remain where the doorway no longer functions as passage.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001653/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_1cb9ab75a833d356753c","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:8:3\",\"root_001653\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Servetin sağlayacağı sanılan kalıcılık, yaşamın sürmesi değil, kapatılmanın kalıcılığı olarak geri döner.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001653/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_243793d1c817963afc6a","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:8:3\",\"root_001653\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Ateşin önce yüreklere girmesi, ardından üzerlerine kapanması, girişi mümkün fakat çıkışı imkânsız kılan tek yönlü bir tuzak kurar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001653/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_34efc851b9343055e0d1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:8:3\",\"root_001653\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Kapanma, koruyup barındıran bir yuva yerine merhametsiz ve çıkışsız bir ters barınak oluşturur.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001653/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_56854b19b01fcbf7f7ec","text":"1. **Sealed permanence replacing imagined permanence.** Evidence: `و ص د B001` (door sealed shut), activated by `خ ل د B001/B002` in 104:3 and `م د د B004` in 104:9. Structural cue: the pronoun points back to the fire/Hutamah after the false immortalizing claim. Assigned role: إيصاد supplies closure and irreversibility. Abductive move: the imagined permanence of wealth is answered by a real permanence of confinement. Reading change: the ayah reads as the conversion of false security into sealed exposure.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001653/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5aa68812759d7198e67b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"104:8:3\",\"root_001653\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Malını elinde tutan kişi, bu kez kendisi kapalı bir ağılda tutulan mülk konumuna düşer.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001653/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5ec9f228e0b802683650","text":"1. **Closure after penetration creates a one-way trap.** Fixed `مُّؤْصَدَةٌ` realizes `و ص د B001`, a door fastened or sealed shut. The immediately prior fire reaches the hearts through `ط ل ع B006` (an access-route) and `ف ء د B003` (a struck heart), while the pronoun and `عَلَيْهِم` place the closure over the occupants. The packet supplies inward access followed by sealing; I infer that entry remains possible precisely where exit is denied. The ayah therefore reads as the valve-stage of the mechanism, not as a static description of a closed place.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001653/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_60258d450a9294ecd826","text":"3. **Anti-house and anti-shelter.** Evidence: `و ص د B002` (forecourt/doorway joined to dwelling) and `ك ل ل B006` (curtain or sewn shelter), activated by `ر ح م B003` in 104:0. Structural cue: enclosure vocabulary at the end answers the opening mercy/womb frame. Assigned role: موصدة supplies dwelling-like closure without hospitality. Abductive move: a shelter can protect or trap depending on the moral relation it encloses. Reading change: the fire is an inverted dwelling: a house with no mercy, door with no exit, covering with no protection.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001653/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_6131bd179b4e3b3b1791","text":"2. **The wealth-keeper becomes penned property.** Evidence: `و ص د B003` (stone pen for livestock/property in the mountain), activated by `م و ل B001` and `ج م ع B001` in 104:2. Structural cue: the enclosure falls \"upon them\" after wealth/property has dominated the human subject. Assigned role: وصيدة provides a property-pen image. Abductive move: one who organized life around مال is placed inside a structure analogous to where مال is kept. Reading change: the human possessor is reread as possessed/penned.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001653/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_92a316c0d09ee05be3d1","text":"4. **Close-rooted confinement.** Evidence: `و ص د B004` (plants with roots growing close together), activated by `ج م ع B001` and `م د د B003` only exploratorily. Structural cue: gathered roots/plants faintly echo gathered wealth and extended water/support images. Assigned role: this branch supplies a compact interlacing image. Abductive move: enclosure may be felt not only as a door but as tight growth with no spacing. The packet supplies pen, driver, property, and people enclosed under `عَلَيْهِم`; I infer the role reversal by which the owner is handled as guarded stock.
